We study this question on a wheeled humanoid platform across two hardware generations, where both the camera and gripper are changed while the overall morphology remains fixed. Contrary to the common assumption that more cross-configuration data is always helpful, we observe a grokking-like transition: legacy data remains ineffective until the upgraded configuration acquires a minimum level of task competence, after which co-training gains rise sharply before diminishing near saturation. We hypothesize that this task-dependent transition is governed by a transfer threshold and characterize the resulting three-phase pattern. Across real-robot manipulation tasks, we observe all three phases: no measurable benefit at low competence ($10.0\% \rightarrow 10.0\%$), a sharp gain after crossing the threshold ($23.3\% \rightarrow 86.7\%$ on flower insertion), and diminishing returns at high competence ($85.0\% \rightarrow 93.3\%$ on pen insertion). We provide a theoretical account based on gradient alignment and residual policy uncertainty, and derive a phase-aware rule for deciding when to collect more new-hardware data and when to reuse legacy demonstrations. We further validate this three-phase pattern on a mobile dual-arm watering task, with results consistent with our predictions.

</details>


### [107] [Shared Voxel-Map-Based Cooperative Indoor UAV Guidance with a Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25728)
*Thomas Hickling,Dylan Wynne,Yu Su,Nabil Aouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合共享体素地图世界模型与多智能体SAC控制器的室内无人机协同制导框架，通过"全局融合、局部决策"的设计实现多无人机空间融合与分布式控制，在仿真中达到90.3%的成功率，并通过离线模仿微调解决了仿真到现实的迁移问题，在GNSS拒止环境下实现了稳定的双机协同运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内无人机协同制导方法面临空间融合与分布式控制难以兼顾的问题，且仿真到现实的迁移仍存在挑战。

Method: 采用共享体素地图作为全局世界模型，多无人机融合360 LiDAR观测构建统一占据地图，转换为紧凑BEV表示后为每个智能体提供局部裁剪。策略结合BEV地图特征、近场障碍观测、目标与同伴状态信息，基于集中训练-分布式执行框架，并使用MASAC算法训练。仿真策略通过离线模仿微调适应真实数据。

Result: 仿真中走廊导航成功率达90.3%，优于A*规划、人工势场控制器及先前引导方法。真实实验中，在GNSS拒止室内环境中实现稳定的双机协同运行，应对逐渐增加的障碍布局挑战。

Conclusion: 共享体素地图表示为学习型室内无人机协同制导提供了有效且可扩展的空间基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a cooperative indoor UAV guidance framework that combines a shared voxel-map world model with a multi-agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC) controller. Multiple drones fuse 360 LiDAR observations into a common world-frame occupancy map, which is converted into a compact bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation and provided to each agent as an ego-aligned local crop. This integrate-in-world, act-in- ego design enables consistent multi-UAV spatial fusion whilst retaining decentralised continuous control. The policy combines BEV map features, near-field obstacle observations, and compact goal and peer-state information within a centralised-training, decentralised-execution framework. In simulation, the learned controller achieves a 90.3% success rate in corridor navigation, outperforming Astar planning, an artificial potential field controller, and a prior guidance method. To address residual sim-to-real mismatch, the simulation-trained policy is further adapted using offline imitation fine-tuning from real-world data. Real-world experiments in GNSS-denied indoor environments demonstrate stable two-UAV cooperative operation across increasingly chal- lenging obstacle layouts. The results show that shared voxel-map representations provide an effective and scalable spatial substrate for learned cooperative indoor UAV guidance.

</details>


### [108] [Tri-Manual Visuomotor Imitation Learning of Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25731)
*James Zhao,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TriManPolicy 提出了一种三臂模仿学习系统，通过依赖感知的三臂调度（DATS）离线重定时演示数据，使单操作员能演示三臂协同任务，解决了双臂操作员与三臂机器人之间控制通道不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双臂遥操作中，操作员与机器人控制通道数量匹配的假设在三臂系统（tri-manual）中不成立：操作员只能同时控制双臂，而机器人需要协调三臂。传统的成对模式切换会导致行为克隆学习到界面引入的延迟而非任务所需的协调。

Method: 提出 TriManPolicy，核心是依赖感知的三臂调度（DATS）。DATS 离线保留固定时长的局部感知运动片段，根据任务顺序和手臂使用约束（由人工审核）重新定位这些片段，从而重定时演示数据，生成同步的三臂策略。

Result: 在六个真实世界挑战性任务中，使用 DATS 重定时后的演示数据训练的策略展现出更高效的协调能力，同时保持可比的观察任务成功率。离线分析进一步表明 DATS 改变了跨手臂的监督信号，而不仅仅是移除空闲时段。

Conclusion: DATS 通过离线重定时有效解决了三臂模仿学习中控制通道不匹配的问题，使单操作员能够演示三臂协同行为，且部署时无需依赖图或调度器。

Abstract: Bimanual teleoperation provides an effective way to collect robot demonstrations, but it assumes that the operator and robot have matching numbers of simultaneous control channels. This assumption breaks for tri-manual systems: the robot can coordinate three arms concurrently, whereas a single operator can continuously control only two. Pairwise mode switching may therefore record otherwise independent motions sequentially, causing behaviour cloning to reproduce delays imposed by the interface rather than required by the task. We present TriManPolicy, a tri-manual imitation learning system that allows one operator to demonstrate behaviours for three arms. Its central component is Dependency-Aware Tri-Arm Scheduling (DATS). The key idea is to preserve the demonstrated arm motions while reconsidering when they occur. DATS retimes demonstrations offline by preserving local sensorimotor segments of fixed duration and repositioning them according to constraints on task order and arm usage that are reviewed by a human. The resulting data train a single synchronous policy for all three arms, while deployment requires neither the dependency graph nor the scheduler. Across six challenging tasks performed in the real world, policies trained on demonstrations retimed by DATS exhibit more efficient coordination while maintaining comparable observed task success. Offline analysis further shows that DATS changes the supervision across arms rather than merely removing idle periods.

</details>


### [109] [Cooperative Multi-UAV Navigation in Complex Environments via Systematic Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25754)
*Yu Su,Nabil Aouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体深度强化学习框架，通过协调探索、演示利用、安全课程调度和结构感知泛化，解决多无人机协同导航中的局部最优、稀疏奖励、学习不平衡和跨场景泛化不足等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机协同导航在复杂环境中面临局部最优陷阱、稀疏奖励、智能体间学习不平衡以及跨场景泛化能力不足等关键挑战，现有方法难以同时有效应对这些问题。

Method: 1) 结合状态记忆、方向新颖度估计和惩罚反向传播的感知机制；2) 分层协作演示缓冲池配合分级行为克隆的演示利用方法；3) 安全感知的双条件课程调度机制，通过回测和经验预填充抑制灾难性遗忘；4) 利用局部几何特征抽象为域参数，通过结构感知门控网络和混合专家机制实现跨场景泛化。

Result: 在混合静态-动态障碍物环境中，该方法在协作成功率、导航鲁棒性、零样本跨场景泛化和动态环境适应性方面均表现出色。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了多无人机协同导航的多重挑战，具备良好的动态扰动适应能力和跨场景迁移能力。

Abstract: Cooperative navigation of multi-agent UAVs in complex environments faces key challenges including local optima traps, sparse rewards, learning imbalance among agents, and insufficient cross-scenario generalisation. This paper proposes a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework that addresses these issues through coordinated exploration, demonstration exploitation, safe curriculum scheduling, and structure-aware generalisation. First, a perception mechanism combining memory of visited states, directional novelty estimates, and penalty backpropagation enables agents to proactively detect and escape local optima. Second, a hierarchical collaborative demonstration buffer with tiered behaviour cloning manages trajectories by degree of team collaboration and applies differential supervision to the actor network, improving demonstration utilisation under sparse collaborative signals. Third, a safety-aware dual-condition curriculum scheduling mechanism reviews mastered scenarios through back-testing and experience pre-filling during training, suppressing catastrophic forgetting while ensuring both task performance and flight safety. For generalisation, local geometric features computed from sensor readings are abstracted into a domain parameter, through which a structure-aware gating network and mixture-of-experts mechanism condition the policy on local structural patterns rather than scenario-specific coordinates, enabling cross-scenario transfer without exposure to the target environment. The framework is further validated under mixed static-dynamic obstacle settings, showing robust adaptability to dynamic disturbances. Simulation results confirm strong performance in collaboration success rate, navigation robustness, zero-shot cross-scenario generalisation, and dynamic environment adaptability.

</details>


### [110] [Tripody: An Overconstrained 3-SPR-like Parallel Robot for High-Reach Construction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25781)
*Julien Kindle,Jakub Raczy,Riccardo Balbi,Andrea Alessandretti,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tripody，一种轮式3自由度并联机器人，用于天花板施工。该机器人采用过约束的3-SPR构型（用万向节替代球铰），通过弹性变形吸收运动不兼容性，实现近似平移运动。系统重33kg，伸展高度1.7-3.4m，可承载32kg。相比传统球铰方案，扭转刚度提升67%-454%，闭环定位精度低于0.6mm，开环钻孔15个孔位最大误差4.5mm。验证了过约束、柔顺吸收的3-SPR类构型在轻量化高空施工机器人中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天花板施工依赖于重型串联机械臂，难以在杂乱室内环境中部署，因此需要轻量化、易部署的替代方案，同时需兼顾毫米级精度和承受高空工具载荷所需的刚度。

Method: 设计Tripody：轮式3-DoF并联机器人，将经典3-SPR构型的基座球铰替换为万向节（过约束设计），利用小型分布式弹性变形吸收运动不兼容性，保留近似平移运动；包含定制直线执行器、运动学兼容性分析、以及结合SE(3)状态估计、正运动学和任务空间控制的控制栈。

Result: 系统重33kg，高度1.7-3.4m，承重32kg；扭转刚度较球铰方案提升67%（1.7m）、196%（2.6m）、454%（3.4m）；闭环定位精度<0.6mm；纯模型外推95百分位误差2.7mm（最大3.6mm）；天花板钻孔15孔最大相对位置误差4.5mm。

Conclusion: 过约束、柔顺吸收的3-SPR类构型是实现轻量化、高空作业、毫米级精度施工机器人的可行路径。

Abstract: Many ceiling construction tasks still rely on heavy serial manipulators that are difficult to deploy in cluttered interiors, motivating lightweight, field-ready alternatives that reach ceiling height while maintaining millimeter-level accuracy and the stiffness demanded by overhead tool loads. We introduce Tripody, a wheeled 3-DoF parallel robot for high-reach tasks that replaces the base spherical joints of a classical 3-SPR (3 legs; S: base spherical joint; P: actuated prismatic joint; R: end-effector revolute joint) morphology with universal joints, intentionally overconstraining the mechanism; small, distributed elastic deflections absorb the resulting incompatibilities, preserving predominantly translational motion. The 33kg system extends from 1.7m to 3.4m in height, supports a continuous 32kg payload, and offers a modular end-effector interface for ceiling operations. We detail the mechanical design - including custom linear actuators and a kinematic-compatibility analysis - and a control stack for accurate positioning that combines SE(3) state estimation, forward kinematics, and task-space control. In experiments, Tripody exhibits similar in-plane stiffness to a spherical-base variant but substantially higher torsional stiffness - an increase of 67% at 1.7m, 196% at 2.6m, and 454% at 3.4m - while maintaining negligible cross-axis coupling. Closed-loop positioning with a total station converges below 0.6mm across the entire workspace; pure model extrapolation achieves a 95th-percentile error of 2.7mm (max 3.6mm). Finally, we demonstrate task-level ceiling-drilling feasibility in an open-loop study by drilling a 15-hole pattern with 4.5mm maximum relative hole-position error after rigid alignment. These results support overconstrained, compliance-absorbing 3-SPR-like architectures as a practical path to lightweight, high- reach, millimeter-accurate construction robots.

</details>


### [111] [Motion-Acceleration Calibration and Compensation in IMUs without External Equipment for Attitude Estimation Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25784)
*Fabian Arzberger,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用陀螺仪测量值补偿IMU因系统运动产生的向心加速度和切向加速度的方法，并引入了一种无需外部设备即可校准IMU内外参数（轴偏差、偏置、缩放因子及基座到IMU向量）的标定方法，显著提升了依赖重力方向的姿态滤波算法在动态系统中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有姿态估计算法（如Madgwick、Mahony）在系统运动时，IMU会受到额外加速度干扰，导致重力测量失真，且IMU离系统基座越远，该效应越明显。尽管这些滤波器在高角速度时依赖陀螺仪积分，但会累积长期误差，尤其绕重力轴方向。因此需要更精确的补偿方法。

Method: 1）利用陀螺仪测量值对运动引起的向心加速度和切向加速度进行补偿；2）提出一种无需外部设备的标定方法，联合估计IMU内部参数（轴偏差、偏置、缩放因子）和外部参数（基座到IMU的位移向量）。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，该方法能改善任何依赖重力方向的姿态滤波器；在高度动态系统及无法将IMU置于旋转中心的系统（如球形移动测绘系统）上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 通过运动学补偿和联合标定，可显著提升动态环境下姿态估计的精度和长期稳定性，且该方法具有通用性，可集成到现有姿态滤波器中。

Abstract: Attitude estimation based on inertial sensing requires measurements of local angular velocities and local gravity via gyroscopes and accelerometers.
  However, during the motion of a mobile system the inertial measurement unit (IMU) will be subject to additional accelerations which skews the measurement of local gravity.
  This effect gets amplified the further away the IMU is from the base of the system.
  Many attitude estimation filters, such as "Madgwick" or "Mahony", account for this by relying more on gyroscope integration for periods of high angular velocity.
  However, this approach is prone to accumulate long term error especially around the gravity vector.
  In this work we utilize the gyroscope measurements to compensate the additional accelerations induced by the motion of the system, i.e., centripetal- and tangential-accelerations.
  Additionally, we introduce a calibration method that estimates intrinsic IMU parameters such as axes misalignment, bias, scale, as well as the extrinsic base-to-IMU vector without the necessity for additional external equipment.
  Our evaluation in simulation as well as in the real-world shows that this method improves any attitude filter that relies on the direction of gravity.
  Furthermore we demonstrate the effectivenes on highly dynamic systems, and systems that are unable to put the IMU at the center of rotation, using our real-world spherical mobile mapping system.

</details>


### [112] [Transformer Transformer: A Unified Model for Motion-Conditioned Robot Co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25798)
*Huy Ha,C. Karen Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer Transformer——一种基于扩散Transformer的机器人协同设计方法，通过RoboTokens统一表征、动力学自引导机制，实现面向目标轨迹的机器人本体生成，零样本优化未见奖励函数，并将ALOHA设计跟踪误差降低70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作性能常受限于本体设计（embodiment），现有方法多关注控制而忽略本体与任务的匹配。作者旨在解决运动条件约束下的机器人协同设计问题，即生成能够跟踪目标末端执行器轨迹并优化用户定义奖励的完整机器人设计。

Method: 提出Transformer Transformer（TT），一种扩散Transformer架构，核心组件包括：(1) RoboTokens——统一的本体、状态和动作令牌化方案；(2) 同一架构可跨本体空间（轮式双臂、四足、人形）和使用场景（本体生成、跨本体控制器）；(3) Dynamics Self-Guidance——将奖励无关的状态/动作预测转化为奖励特定的价值预测，引导本体扩散过程朝向高价值设计。

Result: 在多个设计空间上实现未见奖励和轨迹的零样本优化，性能和运行时间优于进化基线；实际制造的优化ALOHA设计将跟踪误差降低超70%。

Conclusion: 奖励无关的动力学模型结合自引导扩散可高效实现零样本本体设计优化，证明了该方法在跨本体空间泛化和实际制造中的有效性。

Abstract: An often overlooked factor of robot manipulation performance is the embodiment of the robot itself. Motivated by this problem, we study motion-conditioned robot co-design, where the goal is to generate complete robot designs that track target end-effector trajectories (from human demonstrations) while optimizing user-defined rewards. We introduce Transformer Transformer, a diffusion transformer trained on RoboTokens, a unified tokenization of robot embodiments, states, and actions. The same architecture can be used across embodiment spaces (e.g., wheeled bimanual, quadrupeds, humanoids) and use cases (embodiment generation, cross embodiment controller). Rather than overfitting to one reward function, Transformer Transformer is a dynamics model, whose reward-agnostic state and action predictions can be converted into reward-specific value predictions. These value predictions are used to steer embodiment diffusion towards high value robot designs, through a procedure we call Dynamics Self-Guidance. Experiments across multiple design spaces show zero-shot optimization of unseen rewards and trajectories, improving performance and runtime over the evolutionary baseline. Finally, we fabricated an optimized ALOHA design, which reduced tracking error by over 70% compared to the original design.

</details>


### [113] [Modular Robotic Catheters for Endovascular Aneurysm Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25807)
*Alex Ranne,Jinshi Zhao,Ali Anil Demircali,Songli Moey,Ayhan Aktas,Burak Temelkuran,Nassir Navab,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于FEVAR/BEVAR的2段可转向导管系统，采用热纤维拉制和激光微加工制造，通过4自由度增强灵活性，并配有模块化腱驱动平台，仿真和体外实验验证了其弯曲刚度和可转向性，有望缩短手术时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床研究表明，在FEVAR/BEVAR手术中，使用标准商用器械进行血管通路接入仍存在困难，导致手术时间延长并引发更多并发症，因此需要提高导管灵活性和可操控性。

Method: 1) 设计了一种2段式可转向导管，具有4个自由度；2) 开发了可扩展的模块化腱驱动平台以适应系统冗余；3) 采用热纤维拉制技术规模化制造高长径比导管，并通过激光微加工软化导管尖端；4) 通过仿真评估弯曲刚度，并在血管体模中进行体外可转向性实验。

Result: 仿真评估了导管的弯曲刚度，体外血管体模实验验证了导管的可转向性，系统在手持操作下表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 该手持式机器人可转向导管系统有潜力缩短未来血管内手术的时间，并为临床医生提供处理复杂病例的工具。

Abstract: Fenestrated/Branched endovascular aneurysm repair (FEVAR/BEVAR) require surgeons to navigate catheters and guidewires into various branches of the abdominal aorta, before deploying stent grafts to alleviate pressure on the aneurysm. Previous clinical studies suggests that surgeons continue to struggle with vessel access using standard commercial instruments, prolonging the procedural time and inducing further complications. In this work, we present two contributions to solving this problem: 1) A bespoke 2-segment steerable catheter, consisting of 4 degrees of freedom to enhance dexterity. 2) An expandable, modular tendon-driven actuation platform that can accommodate for the redundancies introduced in our system. To fabricate the catheter, we capitalized on thermal fiber drawing, a technique that creates high-aspect ratio devices at scale, and processed the catheter with laser micro-machining to soften its tip. We evaluated the system using simulations, where we investigated the catheter's bending stiffness, then its steerability with in-vitro experiments in vascular phantoms. This handheld, robotic steerable catheter system has the potential to shorten the length of future endovascular surgeries, and give clinicians the tools to resolve challenging clinical cases.

</details>


### [114] [HiFi-UMI: Learning Deployable Manipulation Policies from High-Fidelity UMI Data Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25895)
*Simple AI,:,Yuteng Wei,Jinming Ma,Jiawei Wang,Weitao Zhou,Yushen Zuo,Ke Rui,Minglei Li,Jinhao Zhang,Zhikang Pan,Xiang Wang,Haoran Jia,Huan Du,Zicheng Zeng,Jun Ma,Guiyu Qin,Di Zhang,Xiaofei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiFi-UMI提出了一种高保真、可扩展的机器人免遥操作数据采集系统，通过头戴式立体惯性SLAM、原生相对位姿、微秒级GPIO触发和超广角相机（~200°视野），实现了3mm精度的末端执行器定位。该系统使得策略仅使用HiFi-UMI数据进行后训练即可直接部署在真实机器人上，在多个VLA和世界动作模型骨干上性能与遥操作基线相当（成功率差距-2.5到+3.1个百分点），最强策略在精密插入任务上达85%。4000小时预训练将10个未见任务的动作误差降低41%，并进一步提升18.1个百分点。开源了2000小时微秒同步、超广角视野的HiFi-UMI-2K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人遥操作数据保真度高但扩展性差，而机器人免捕获（UMI）数据可规模扩展但保真度不足，导致当前范式需要通过少量真实机器人数据做后训练"锚定"。本文的目标是提升UMI数据的保真度，消除对真实机器人后训练数据的依赖。

Method: 设计了HiFi-UMI系统：1）头戴式离线立体惯性SLAM进行轨迹估计；2）使用原生而非重构的相对位姿；3）共享微秒级GPIO触发实现多相机同步；4）每只手配备两个超广角相机覆盖~200°视野。实现了无需外部追踪设施的3mm工作空间局部末端执行器精度。

Result: 零机器人后训练：在StarVLA-QwenPI、OpenPI-pi_0.5、LingBot-VA上成功率差异分别为-2.5、+3.1、-0.6个百分点；最强策略在精密插入任务达85%。4000小时预训练降低10个未见任务动作误差41%，在StarVLA-QwenPI上真实机器人成功率提升18.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过提升免遥操作数据采集系统的保真度，可以消除对真实机器人后训练数据的依赖，实现高保真、大规模可扩展的机器人学习数据生产。开源HiFi-UMI-2K数据集（2000小时微秒同步、超广角FOV演示）可作为机器人学习社区的大规模高保真资源。

Abstract: Learning deployable manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of data that is both high-fidelity and scalable. Real-robot teleoperation is accurate but costly to scale; robot-free UMI capture scales readily, and current practice uses the resulting data mainly for pre-training, adding a small real-robot "anchor" at post-training. We ask whether raising the fidelity of robot-free UMI data, rather than shrinking the real-robot fraction, can remove that anchor. We present HiFi-UMI, a portable UMI data-production system co-designed for trajectory accuracy, inter-gripper relative pose, synchronization, and field of view: head-mounted offline stereo-inertial SLAM, native rather than reconstructed relative pose, a shared microsecond GPIO trigger, and two wide-angle cameras per hand covering ~200 degrees. It reaches 3 mm workspace-local end-effector accuracy without external tracking infrastructure. Using this corpus, we demonstrate zero-robot post-training: a policy post-trained solely on HiFi-UMI demonstrations deploys directly on a real robot and matches in-domain teleoperation across three backbones spanning the vision-language-action and world-action-model families, with success-rate differences of -2.5, +3.1, and -0.6 percentage points on StarVLA-QwenPI, OpenPI-pi_0.5, and LingBot-VA; the strongest policy reaches 85% on a precision insertion task, even though the teleoperation baseline is collected in the evaluation scene and no HiFi-UMI trajectory is. Pre-training on 4,000 hours from the same corpus lowers action error on ten unseen tasks by 41% and, on StarVLA-QwenPI, raises real-robot success by a further 18.1 percentage points. We open-source HiFi-UMI-2K, 2,000 hours of microsecond-synchronized, ultra-wide-FoV demonstrations, each automatically reconstructed and validated through simulation replay, as a large-scale, high-fidelity resource for the robot-learning community.

</details>


### [115] [SAM3D-Guided Object-Centric Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25912)
*Zonghe Liu,Shanyuan Jie,Xiaoquan Sun,Chen Cao,Zetian Xu,Zongsheng Liu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于π₀的目标中心3D表示对齐框架，利用SAM3D作为冻结的3D教师模型，在训练时将目标物体的3D先验对齐到π₀的中间视觉特征中，从而在不增加测试时计算负担的情况下提升VLA模型的3D理解与操作能力，在LIBERO上达到99.1%成功率，CALVIN上平均任务长度为4.11，在长时域多子任务场景中表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型多基于2D视觉-语言骨干网络，缺乏对目标物体的细粒度3D理解，尤其在遮挡、姿态变化、尺度变化及精确空间交互场景下表现不佳。

Method: 基于π₀框架，使用SAM3D作为冻结的3D教师模型，通过目标识别定位任务相关物体并生成掩码，利用SAM3D提取密集的物体级3D表示，并与π₀的中间视觉特征进行对齐，使策略内化目标物体的3D信息，同时保持原有的RGB-语言-动作推理流程，测试时不需深度图、点云、掩码、SAM3D或额外3D模块。

Result: 仿真实验：LIBERO任务上达到99.1%成功率，CALVIN上平均任务长度为4.11。真实世界实验证明该方法在需要跨多个子任务关注不同目标物体的长时域操作场景中尤其有效。

Conclusion: 通过训练阶段的3D表示对齐，可以在不增加测试时计算开销的前提下显著提升VLA模型在复杂3D操作场景中的表现，特别适用于长时域多目标操作任务。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general robot manipulation, but most existing models rely on 2D visual-language backbones and lack fine-grained 3D understanding of target objects, especially under occlusion, pose variation, scale changes, and precise spatial interaction. We propose an object-centric 3D representation alignment framework built upon $π_0$, using SAM3D as a frozen 3D teacher to provide target-object 3D priors during training. Specifically, we localize task-relevant objects with object recognition models, generate corresponding object masks, and use SAM3D to extract dense object-level 3D representations, which are aligned with intermediate visual features of $π_0$. This enables the policy to internalize target-object 3D information while preserving the original RGB-language-to-action inference pipeline without requiring depth, point clouds, masks, SAM3D, or additional 3D modules at test time. Simulation experiments show consistent improvements, achieving 99.1\% on LIBERO and an average length of 4.11 on CALVIN. Real-world experiments further demonstrate that our method is particularly effective in long-horizon manipulation scenarios where the robot must focus on different target objects across multiple subtasks.

</details>


### [116] [DC-WAM: Dynamic-Centric Visual Supervision and Reasoning for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25918)
*Haoyuan Ji,Lingxiang Fan,Shang Su,Yinqiao Lu,Mengkai Shi,Jun Gao,Shuo Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DC-WAM提出了一种以动态为中心的World-Action Models框架，通过时序差分流匹配和轨迹引导加权重分配RGB视频分支的监督信号，并结合DynaRoute动态相关性路由机制引导模型关注控制相关的未来token，在模拟和真实操作任务中提升了策略性能，尤其在光照、物体外观和背景纹理的分布外扰动下表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World-Action Models（WAMs）使用未来视觉预测增强机器人策略，但视觉模态的学习目标不明确：光度逼真的未来预测虽然提供密集监督，但计算量大且将模型容量分配给了与动作选择弱相关的纹理、光照和背景变化。近期高效的WAM变体表明，视频分支的主要益处可能不在于渲染的未来本身，而在于训练过程中诱导出的与控制相关的视觉表征。

Method: 提出DC-WAM框架，包含两个层面的改进：1）监督层面，结合时序差分流匹配（temporal-difference flow matching）与轨迹引导加权（trajectory-guided weighting），强调密集的时间变化以及夹爪、操作物体和接触区域运动的局部区域；2）推理层面，提出DynaRoute预测token级别的动态相关性并将其转换为注意力偏置，引导模型关注与控制相关的未来token。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，DC-WAM一致性地提升了策略性能，尤其在光照、物体外观和背景纹理等分布外扰动条件下表现更为突出。

Conclusion: DC-WAM通过重新分配RGB视频分支的监督和计算资源，将WAM从外观主导的重建导向交互引发的视觉动态，无需引入额外的模态特定预测或在线输入，即可在部署时显著提升策略的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies with future visual prediction, but it remains unclear what the visual modality should learn for control. While photorealistic future prediction provides dense supervision, it also incurs substantial computation and can allocate capacity to texture, illumination, and background variations that are only weakly related to action selection. Recent efficient WAM variants suggest that the main benefit of the video branch may not lie in the rendered future itself, but in the control-relevant visual representations induced during training. In this work, we revisit future video prediction from a dynamic-centric perspective and ask whether an existing RGB-based WAM can be redirected from appearance-dominated reconstruction toward interaction-induced visual dynamics without introducing additional modality-specific predictions or online inputs at deployment. We propose DC-WAM, a dynamic-centric WAM framework that redistributes supervision and computation in the RGB video branch. At the supervision level, DC-WAM combines temporal-difference flow matching with trajectory-guided weighting, emphasizing dense temporal changes and localized regions where the gripper, manipulated objects, and contact areas move. At the reasoning level, DynaRoute predicts token-wise dynamic relevance and converts it into an attention bias, guiding the model toward control-relevant future tokens. Experiments in simulation and on real-world manipulation tasks show that DC-WAM consistently improves policy performance, especially under out-of-distribution perturbations in lighting, object appearance, and background texture.

</details>


### [117] [Physics-Aware End-to-End Deep Reinforcement Learning for Quadcopter Control with Actuator Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25985)
*Ya-Chia Shen,Woei-Leong Chan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于物理感知的端到端深度强化学习（DRL）方法，直接以总推力和机体扭矩作为低层控制输入，通过高保真Simulink环境（包含12状态刚体模型、Action2RPM分配及一阶执行器动力学）实现四旋翼无人机自主控制。评估了DDPG、TD3、PPO、SAC四种算法，发现SAC和TD3在稳定性和探索效率上表现最优，而PPO样本效率较低。研究强调了建模执行器滞后和气动力矩对稳定低层控制的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机因仅有四个控制输入却需控制六自由度（欠驱动系统），其自主控制面临独特挑战。现有研究缺乏对低层物理输入（推力和扭矩）的端到端DRL控制及执行器动力学影响的深入分析。

Method: 构建基于Simulink的高保真仿真环境，集成12状态刚体模型（MATLAB Level-2 S-Function）、基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的Action2RPM分配矩阵、一阶执行器动力学（时间常数0.076s）及转子陀螺耦合；设计含指数位置阱、姿态惩罚和二次速度代价的成形奖励函数；在悬停和带俯仰扭矩的平移悬停两阶段评测DDPG、TD3、PPO、SAC四种算法。

Result: SAC和TD3在稳定性和探索效率上显著优于DDPG和PPO；PPO样本效率最低；执行器滞后和空气动力矩建模对实现稳定的低层控制至关重要。

Conclusion: 物理感知的端到端DRL方法能够有效处理四旋翼的欠驱动控制问题，其中SAC和TD3是较优的算法选择；该工作为四旋翼DRL控制提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), particularly quadcopters, present unique challenges for autonomous control due to their underactuated dynamics: only four available control inputs must govern six degrees of freedom. This paper investigates a physics-aware, end-to-end deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach that acts directly on low-level body inputs, total thrust and body torques $(T, τ_x, τ_y, τ_z)$, and closes the loop through a high-fidelity Simulink environment. Our simulator integrates a 12-state rigid-body model (MATLAB Level-2 S-Function) with (i) an Action2RPM allocation based on the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse of a coefficient matrix derived from thrust and drag terms, and (ii) first-order actuator dynamics for each motor (time constant $T_m = 0.076$ s), including rotor gyroscopic coupling. A shaped reward balances goal-reaching and stability using an exponential position well, attitude penalties, and quadratic velocity costs. Four DRL algorithms, DDPG, TD3, PPO, and SAC, are evaluated in two stages: (S1) thrust-only hover and (S2) hover with pitch torque and a translated goal. Results show that SAC and TD3 achieve superior stability and exploration efficiency, while PPO is less sample-efficient. The study highlights the significance of modeling actuator lags and aerodynamic moments for stable low-level control and provides a reproducible benchmark for quadcopter DRL.

</details>


### [118] [S2A2: Audio-Visual Imitation Learning for Manipulation Tasks Using Acoustic Spatial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26047)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Benjamin Yen,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向声学感知操作任务的多模态模仿学习框架S2A2，该框架融合视觉、声学空间和声学信号信息，使机器人能够通过听觉线索定位和识别操作目标。在仿真和真实机器人实验中验证了该方法在需要位置和音色信息的任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声学信息包含丰富的物体位置、材质属性及接触/运动变化线索，但现有机器人操作研究主要依赖视觉信息，缺乏对听觉线索的有效利用。作者希望填补这一空白，使机器人能够像人类一样利用听觉信息辅助操作决策。

Method: 提出S2A2（Spatial-Spectral Audio Action）多模态模仿学习框架，将视觉特征与声学空间信息（空间定位）和声学信号信息（频谱特征）进行融合。该框架支持集成多种策略网络，包括ACT、Diffusion Policy、VQ-BeT和π₀。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法在同时需要位置和音色信息的任务中表现最优；真实机器人实验验证了所提任务和框架在现实操作场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 融合声学空间与频谱信息的模仿学习框架能够有效解决声学感知操作任务，为机器人利用听觉线索进行自主探索和操作提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Acoustic information provides rich cues about object location, material properties, and changes caused by contact or motion. This paper introduces a new set of acoustic-aware manipulation tasks for imitation learning, in which robots must use auditory cues to determine manipulation targets. These tasks require sound source localization and identification for active exploration in robotic manipulation. Also, we propose a multimodal imitation learning framework, Spatial-Spectral Audio Action (S2A2), that integrates visual features with acoustic spatial and acoustic signal information for the acoustic-aware manipulation tasks. We implemented S2A2 models that integrates policies such as ACT, Diffusion Policy, VQ-BeT, and $π_0$, into our framework. Simulation experiments showed that the proposed method is the most effective for tasks requiring both position and timbre. Furthermore, real-robot experiments confirm the applicability of the proposed tasks and framework to real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [119] [$π\mathbf{R}^2$: Reactive Real-time Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26055)
*Sungjae Park,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: πR²通过快速本体感知通道与异步慢速视觉通道的解耦，以及延迟自适应流调度，使基于大模型的动作分块流策略（如GR00T-N1.7）具备实时反应能力，在保持大骨干网络和多模态策略的同时，实现约25Hz的闭环重规划（4倍加速），仿真和真实操作成功率分别提升23%和30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大预训练骨干网络的动作分块流策略采用开环执行方式，在动作块执行期间无法响应中间到达的感官输入，牺牲了反应性。虽然更频繁地重规划可以恢复反应性，但感知到动作的流水线（大骨干+多步去噪）延迟过高，无法支持频繁重规划，使得此类策略不适合动态闭环控制场景。

Method: πR²基于扩散强迫的逐位置噪声调度，提出两个核心思想：1) 将条件输入分为快速通道（本体感知，每tick更新）和异步慢速通道（视觉-语言特征），使策略在动作块内能响应本体感知变化同时容忍视觉信息延迟；2) 延迟自适应流调度，将执行中的动作视为修复条件，每次调用仅需单步去噪即可生成动作，使单一训练模型能适应不同硬件延迟。该方法只需对现有架构做最小修改，可从预训练策略微调而来。

Result: 将πR²应用于GR00T-N1.7，在真实xArm6+XHand平台上实现约25Hz的闭环重规划（基于A5000 GPU），每40ms处理一次新观测，相比基线策略提速约4倍。在仿真和真实操作任务中，成功率分别比最强基线提升23%和30%。

Conclusion: πR²有效解决了基于大模型的动作分块流策略的反应性与延迟问题，通过条件解耦和延迟自适应调度，在保留大骨干网络和表达能力的同时实现了实时闭环控制，显著提升了操作成功率。

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies increasingly take the form of action-chunking flow policies built on large pretrained backbones. Such chunks run open-loop, so the policy cannot react to sensory input arriving mid-execution, sacrificing \emph{reactivity}. Replanning more often would restore it, but the perception-to-action pipeline (a large backbone plus multiple denoising steps) is too slow: this \emph{latency} forbids frequent replanning and leaves committed actions stale, making such policies ill-suited for dynamic, closed-loop control. We present $π\mathbf{R}^2$, which makes these policies reactive and real-time while retaining large backbones, expressive multi-modal policies, and multi-action prediction. Built on the per-position noise schedule of diffusion forcing, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ contributes two ideas. First, it splits conditioning into a fast channel (proprioception, fresh every tick) and an asynchronously updated slow channel (vision-language features), so the policy reacts to proprioception within a chunk while tolerating stale vision. Second, a latency-adaptive flow schedule treats in-flight actions as inpainting conditioning and emits actions in one denoising step per call, letting one trained model adapt to varying hardware latency. Requiring minimal modification to existing architectures, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ can be finetuned from a pretrained policy: applied to GR00T-N1.7 on a real xArm6+XHand platform, it replans closed-loop roughly $4\times$ faster than the base policy (~$25$Hz on an A5000 GPU), acting on a fresh observation every $40$ms. Across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ improves the success rate by up to $23\%$ in simulation and $30\%$ in the real world over the strongest baseline. Project page: https://pi-r2-flow.github.io/

</details>


### [120] [INTACT: Isomorphic Intent-to-Action Learning for Search-Free World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26056)
*Junhan Sun,Hao Zhao,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INTACT是一个端到端的联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)，将无奖励的动作标记轨迹转化为可直接部署的意图到动作接口。它通过四槽语法和共享参数的等构架构、非逐点潜变量等价的行动律语义、非对称端点梯度等技术，在四个LeWM任务上实现零搜索、单epoch训练达到85.78%-100.00%的成功率，直接推理仅需2.9-5.5ms，且可将CEM采样量减少23.44倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向潜世界模型需要昂贵的测试时搜索才能将期望场景变化转化为具体动作，缺乏高效的意图到动作的直接映射机制。作者希望构建一个无需测试时搜索、端到端可学习的意图到动作接口。

Method: 1) 基于JEPA架构，每个变换提供物理意图(z_{t+1}-z_t)，未来目标提供部署意图(sg(z_g)-z_t)；2) 局部和目标的运动意图主干-输入图通过四槽语法和共享参数保持等构；3) 通过同一预测器诱导行动律语义而非逐点潜变量等价；4) 非对称端点梯度用于锚定物理后继和固定未来目标；5) 条件均值作为无搜索策略，保留采样能力用于多样性。

Result: 在四个LeWM任务上，零搜索模型分别达到85.78%、100.00%、97.67%、97.89%的成功率。可选局部CEM使用384个候选序列（而非9000个）达到96.86%宏平均成功率，采样量减少23.44倍，比纯CEM提升16.00个百分点。单共享编码器在四个任务上达到89.39%的E5 Direct宏平均，每项任务均优于联合训练的LeWM。预测-专家动作族kNN与Direct成功率的相关系数r=0.954。直接推理耗时2.9-5.5ms。

Conclusion: INTACT通过等构架构和非对称端点梯度成功将表示学习与控制相结合，无需逐点潜变量匹配或全局线性动力学假设，在效率和性能上均显著优于现有方法，为意图到动作的端到端映射提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Forward latent world models predict how actions change a scene, but recover actions for a desired change only through expensive test-time search. We introduce INTACT (INtent-To-ACTion), an end-to-end JEPA that turns action-labeled, reward-free trajectories into a deployable intent-to-action interface. Each transition supplies physical intent $z_{t+1}-z_t$, while a future goal supplies deployment intent $\operatorname{sg}(z_g)-z_t$. The architecture is isomorphic between the local and goal motion-intent backbone-input graphs through an identical four-slot grammar and shared parameters, and between supported local and goal motion-intent families through action-law semantics induced by the same predictor rather than pointwise latent equality. INTACT also provides intact transfer from RGB evidence to action-effective latent intent coordinates and from intent families to their corresponding action-law families. Asymmetric endpoint gradients ground physical successors and fix future goals as anchors, joining representation learning and control without pointwise latent matching or globally linear dynamics. The resulting coordinates support a robust distributional action law: its conditional mean serves directly as a search-free policy, while sampling remains available for diversity or optional verification. On the four official LeWM tasks, one-epoch, zero-search models reach 85.78\%, 100.00\%, 97.67\%, and 97.89\% success. Optional local CEM centered on the Direct plan reaches 96.86\% macro success using 384 instead of 9,000 candidate sequences, reducing sampling by $23.44\times$ while improving pure CEM by 16.00 points. One shared four-task encoder reaches 89.39\% E5 Direct macro and improves every task over jointly trained LeWM, while predicted--expert action-family kNN tracks Direct success at $r=0.954$. Direct inference takes 2.9--5.5 ms.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [FinAbstain: Uncertainty-Calibrated Multimodal RAG for Selective Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24875)
*Dorothy Torres,Wei Cheng,Henan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FinAbstain是一个面向金融预测的不确定性校准多模态检索增强生成框架，通过选择性预测机制——在不确定性超过阈值时主动弃权——来平衡预测覆盖率与预测精度，降低选择性错误和回撤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在金融叙事生成中常因证据稀疏、过时或矛盾而表现出过度自信，这在多模态金融信号（财报、新闻、价格、成交量、技术指标等）不一致时尤为严重，可能导致错误预测和实际交易损失。

Method: 提出FinAbstain框架，包含：(1) 时间点感知检索器，仅获取预测时刻前公开的信息；(2) 多模态专家代理（基本面、新闻、技术、风险、验证代理）分别产出概率评估；(3) 聚合检索相关性、证据矛盾度、重复采样一致性、历史校准统计；(4) 评估温度缩放、等渗回归、共形预测及提出的混合不确定性分数；(5) 控制器在不确定性低于阈值时输出预测，否则弃权、请求证据、降低敞口或转人工审核。

Result: 报告了显式标注的模拟结果（非实证结果）：校准后的弃权机制通过牺牲覆盖率换取更低的选样错误率和回撤。评估涵盖1天/5天异常收益率方向预测、20天波动率区间预测及弃权决策，使用准确率、校准度、风险-覆盖率、引用、交易、延迟和成本等指标。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出了一种时间安全的架构、一种复合不确定性公式，以及一个可复现的、基于证据的选择性金融预测评估蓝图，为后续完整数据收集前的审计提供了设计框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can synthesize financial narratives but may express high confidence when evidence is sparse, stale, or contradictory. This failure is especially consequential in forecasting, where filings, news, prices, volume, and technical signals can disagree. We present FinAbstain, a research framework for uncertainty-calibrated multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with selective prediction. A point-in-time retriever admits only information public at the forecast timestamp and supplies modality-specific evidence to fundamental, news, technical, risk, and verification agents. Their probabilistic assessments are aggregated with retrieval relevance, evidence contradiction, repeated-sample consistency, and historical calibration statistics. Temperature scaling, isotonic regression, conformal prediction, and a proposed hybrid uncertainty score are evaluated under a common chronological protocol. A controller predicts bullish, bearish, or neutral outcomes only when uncertainty is below a validated threshold; otherwise it abstains, requests evidence, reduces exposure, or routes the case to human review. The evaluation covers one- and five-day abnormal-return direction, twenty-day volatility intervals, and abstention decisions, using accuracy, calibration, risk--coverage, citation, trading, latency, and cost metrics. To make the design auditable before a full data collection is complete, we report explicitly labeled simulated results rather than empirical claims. These results illustrate the intended hypothesis: calibrated abstention may trade coverage for lower selective error and drawdown. The contribution is a time-safe architecture, a composite uncertainty formulation, and a reproducible evaluation blueprint for evidence-grounded selective financial forecasting.

</details>


### [122] [Human Preference aligned Tabular Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24880)
*Frederik Hoppe,Astrid Franz,Marianne Michaelis,Lars Kleinemeier,Udo Göbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前任务无关的表格嵌入方法主要针对预测任务优化，而非生成符合人类偏好的相似性排序，在PLM等实际业务中缺乏可信度评估。作者提出了一种结合人类偏好对齐的评估流程，并通过PLM用例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格嵌入评估标准主要关注预测任务的表现，而忽略了在相似性搜索场景中人类偏好对齐的重要性，导致推荐结果可能不符合用户实际需求。

Method: 提出一种具体的评估流程，该流程将人类偏好对齐纳入相似性搜索的评估体系，并通过产品生命周期管理(PLM)的实际用例进行问题说明和验证。

Result: 通过PLM用例展示了标准下游指标无法全面评估嵌入在相似性搜索中的可信度，而引入人类偏好对齐的评估能够揭示现有方法的不足。

Conclusion: 对于任务无关的表格嵌入在相似性搜索中的应用，人类偏好对齐的评估是必要且目前缺失的环节，所提评估流程能更可靠地衡量嵌入的实用性。

Abstract: Task-agnostic tabular embeddings are increasingly used for similarity search in real-world business systems such as Product Lifecycle Management (PLM). However, leading embedding approaches are optimized primarily for prediction tasks - not for producing human preference aligned similarity rankings. We argue that standard downstream metrics are insufficient to fully assess embedding trustworthiness for similarity search and that human preference aligned evaluation is a necessary and currently missing component. We present a concrete evaluation procedure and illustrate the problem through a PLM use case.

</details>


### [123] [Behavior-Driven Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24881)
*Caroline Dominik,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出行为驱动可解释性（BDX）方法，利用行为驱动开发（BDD）的结构化场景直接生成系统行为的解释，并通过RISC-V处理器异常解释的案例研究验证了该方法在系统设计阶段的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着系统复杂性大幅增加，单个人员或团队难以完全理解整个系统的所有方面，尤其是在系统开发生命周期的不同阶段（如设计或维护）。对于安全关键系统，最终设计必须可信，因此可解释性成为现代系统的重要需求。

Method: 利用行为驱动开发（BDD），以结构化场景的形式给出预期系统行为，每个功能对应一系列动作序列，这些场景可直接转化为解释。作者将此方法称为行为驱动可解释性（BDX），适用于任何开发阶段或抽象级别。

Result: 通过RISC-V处理器中异常解释的案例研究，展示了BDX概念在系统设计阶段提供的支持。

Conclusion: BDX方法能够在系统开发各阶段基于规范生成可解释性，增强了安全关键系统的可信度，案例研究验证了其在设计阶段的实用价值。

Abstract: As system complexity has vastly increased, it has become significantly more challenging for a single person or a team to fully understand all aspects of an entire system. Particularly, this holds when considering all the different stages of a system's development life cycle, such as, e.g., design or maintenance. But especially for safety-critical systems it is essential that the final design can be trusted. Because of this, explainability is becoming an important requirement for modern systems. In this paper, we aim to achieve this goal by utilizing Behavior-Driven Development (BDD), where the expected system behavior is given in the form of structured scenarios. These scenarios give a sequence of actions for each functionality, and by this can be directly translated into explanations. We introduce this method of deriving explanations based on the specification as Behavior-Driven Explainability (BDX). While applicable at any development stage or abstraction level, a case study for the explanation of exceptions in a RISC-V processor shows the support this concept adds during system design.

</details>


### [124] [Eliminating Propagation Delay: Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24885)
*Jinpeng Chen,Ziyu Yu,Tao Wang,Jun Ma,Hongbo Gao,Senzhang Wang,Zufeng Zhang,Kaimin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注意力机制的时空融合图卷积网络(A-STFGCN)，通过消除传播延迟误差和融合多头自注意力机制，在五个真实数据集上实现了最优的交通流预测性能，同时保持了较高的计算和数据利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图卷积模型主要关注拓扑结构中的静态空间依赖和时空相关性，忽略了相邻节点间信息传播延迟差异，且复杂结构导致训练计算量大、时效性差。

Method: 设计时空融合块消除传播延迟误差以提取时空特征相关性；引入基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制捕获数据的长期和短期时间特征。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，与八种基线方法相比，A-STFGCN在整体性能最优的同时具有良好的计算和数据利用效率。

Conclusion: A-STFGCN有效解决了传播延迟和计算效率问题，为交通流预测提供了一种更高效、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting traffic flow is crucial to optimizing transportation systems and improving urban mobility. Many graph convolution-based models have been proposed to extract spatial-temporal features and predict traffic flow. However, most focus on spatial-temporal and semantic correlation in topological relationships. There are two primary problems to address. Firstly, the convolutional structure in the model focuses on utilizing static spatial dependencies and spatial-temporal relationships in topological structures, while neglecting the different information propagation delays between adjacent nodes in the convolution. Secondly, these methods often stack a large number of complex structures, resulting in a substantial increase in computational time during the model training phase, thereby disregarding the model's requirements for timeliness. In this paper, we propose a novel network called the Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network (A-STFGCN). We design a spatial-temporal fusion block to extract the spatial-temporal feature correlations with propagation delay errors removed and to capture both long-term and short-term temporal characteristics of the data within a multi-head self-attention mechanism based on a mask matrix. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves the best overall performance while having good computation and data utilization efficiency compared with the eight baseline methods.

</details>


### [125] [Mechanisms of Width Scaling in Normalized Residual Networks: The Effective Alignment Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24887)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Guangming Tan,Fangming Liu,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对函数保持的残差扩展问题，提出了有效对齐维度这一可测量量，用于描述激活梯度的信号-噪声几何结构。通过推导独立估计的训练与测试梯度内积的精确均值和方差，得到了误对齐概率的有限样本上界。该上界仅依赖于有效对齐维度和有效样本量，无需协方差谱假设或指定宽度增长率。实验验证了更宽的模型具有更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验误对齐，且对齐统计量可预测留出损失变化的符号和幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络宽度理论主要刻画渐近极限，但对于有限训练数据中识别的扩展方向能否在未见数据上保持有效，缺乏实际指导。

Method: 提出了有效对齐维度（effective alignment dimension）描述激活梯度的信号-噪声几何结构；推导了独立估计的训练与测试梯度内积的精确均值和方差，得到误对齐概率的有限样本上界（仅需有限二阶矩和非零总体梯度，无需协方差谱假设或宽度增长率）；将这一保证集成到训练-测试残差扩展框架中，得到测试风险改进的高概率条件。

Result: 在宽度可控的LLaMA风格Transformer、Pythia和ResNet-20上的实验表明：更宽模型具有更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验误对齐；直接残差干预实验证实对齐统计量可预测留出损失变化的符号和幅度。

Conclusion: 有效对齐维度为评估残差扩展方向在测试集上的有效性提供了可操作的有限样本保证，揭示了更宽的模型在分布外泛化方面的优势。

Abstract: Existing theories of neural-network width characterize asymptotic limits, but provide limited guidance on whether an expansion direction identified from finite training data remains beneficial on unseen data. We study this problem for function-preserving residual expansion and introduce the effective alignment dimension, a measurable quantity describing the signal-noise geometry of activation gradients. By deriving the exact mean and variance of the inner product between independently estimated training and test gradients, we obtain a finite-sample upper bound on misalignment probability. The bound depends only on the effective alignment dimension and an effective sample size, requiring finite second moments and a nonzero population gradient, without covariance spectral assumptions or prescribed width-growth rates. We integrate this certificate into the train-test residual-expansion framework, yielding a high-probability condition for test-risk improvement. Experiments across width-controlled LLaMA-style Transformers, Pythia, and ResNet-20 show that wider models exhibit larger effective alignment dimensions and lower empirical misalignment. Direct residual interventions confirm that the alignment statistic predicts the sign and magnitude of held-out loss changes.

</details>


### [126] [GAUGE: Grading Agent-Built Financial Models Without a Golden Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24889)
*Jiacheng Lu,Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Rui Sun,Cheng Hua,Tao Song,Hui Cai,Beidi Luan,Zhengze Wu,Lingjing Teng,Yijia He,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GAUGE基准，用于评估基于代理的估值模型。与传统依赖单一专家参考的评分方式不同，GAUGE基于观察到的分析师实践，使用1001份分析师工作簿和196项评估任务，通过三层实践包络、56个可审计维度、八个有效性门控和确定性结构检查来评估模型。研究发现当前最优代理得分53.4，高于学生均值但低于所有资深分析师，表明代理在模型构建上强于估值判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融模型基准通常将输出与单一专家参考进行对比评分，但现实中不同分析师对同一公司的预测、折现率和目标价存在合理分歧。作者发现108对独立分析师模型中，中位数单一参考评分仅0.33，92.6%低于0.70，且不存在同一年份的分析师对在隐含价格上达成10%以内的一致。点容差评分会惩罚专业人员之间已有的分歧，需要新的评估方法。

Method: GAUGE基准包含：1001份供应商分类的分析师工作簿、196项评估任务、三层观察实践包络、56个可审计维度、八个有效性门控和确定性结构检查。通过55名参与者的已知群体研究、公司分组交叉验证和评判者稳定性审计进行验证。采用失败感知评分φ₀进行评估。

Result: 资深分析师平均φ₀得分为88.3，初级分析师66.0，金融学生43.2。24个代理和1011次生成中，最佳代理得分53.4，高于学生均值但低于所有资深分析师和大多数初级分析师。最佳代理通过93%的机械性方面和78%的判断性方面，群组中位数差距为26分。当前代理在模型构建方面明显强于估值判断。

Conclusion: 现有代理在金融建模中已具备一定能力（尤其在模型构建方面），但在需要专业判断的估值任务上仍有显著差距。GAUGE基准为评估代理在金融建模中的表现提供了更符合实际实践的标准。作者公开了方法论、脱敏数据、训练集、48项评估核心和预留刷新池。

Abstract: Financial models combine public disclosures with analyst assumptions to produce forecasts and valuations. While some components can be checked mechanically, forecasts, discount rates, and target prices often admit multiple reasonable answers. Existing benchmarks nevertheless tend to grade such outputs against a single expert reference. Using independently built analyst models for the same companies, we find that across 108 directed pairs covering 65 companies, the median single-reference score is 0.33, 92.6% score below 0.70, and no same-vintage pair agrees on implied price within 10%. Point-tolerance grading can therefore penalize disagreement already present among professionals. We introduce GAUGE, a benchmark for evaluating agent-built valuation models against observed analyst practice rather than a single point answer. GAUGE uses 1,001 vendor-classified analyst workbooks and a 196-task evaluation set, with a three-layer observed-practice envelope, 56 auditable facets, eight validity gates, and deterministic structural checks. We validate the benchmark with a 55-participant known-groups study, company-grouped cross-fitting, and judge-stability audits. On the failure-aware score $φ_0$, senior analysts average 88.3, juniors 66.0, and finance students 43.2. Across 24 agents and 1,011 scored generations, the best agent scores 53.4, above the student mean but below every senior and most juniors. It passes 93% of mechanical facets and 78% of judgment facets, with a fleet-median gap of 26 points. Current agents are substantially stronger at model construction than valuation judgment. We release the methodology, a gated de-identified data tier, a controlled training split, a versioned 48-task evaluation core, and a withheld refresh pool.

</details>


### [127] [LLM as Forecasting Planner: Training-Free Text Conditioning for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24892)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAFP（LLM作为预测规划器），一种无需训练的方法，将冻结的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）作为模拟器生成数值轨迹，LLM作为策略和价值函数，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在预测时间线上进行搜索，以在无需重新训练的情况下实现文本条件下的时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件的时间序列预测需要同时具备可靠的数值预测能力和文本上下文理解能力。TSFM擅长数值预测，LLM擅长文本推理，但直接将两者结合会导致LLM生成的预测值扭曲TSFM捕获的时间结构。现有方法缺乏有效的、无需训练的跨模态融合方案。

Method: 将预测任务建模为TSFM生成轨迹上的规划问题。冻结TSFM作为模拟器（Simulator）生成数值延续序列，LLM分为两个角色：Ranker LLM作为策略（Policy）在搜索过程中引导候选序列选择，Judge LLM作为价值函数（Value Function）评估完整轨迹与上下文的一致性。采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在预测时间线上进行搜索，整个过程无需训练任何模型。

Result: 在Context-is-Key和Time-MMD基准上，使用两种TSFM主干（Chronos和TimesFM）和四种LLM进行实验，LAFP在所有模型组合下均实现了一致的改进，验证了序列搜索作为文本条件预测的有效无需训练方法。

Conclusion: LAFP通过将LLM作为规划器而非直接数值生成器，成功弥合了TSFM与LLM之间的模态鸿沟，提供了一种训练无关、即插即用的文本条件时间序列预测框架。

Abstract: Text-conditioned time-series forecasting predicts a series from both its numerical history and natural-language context, allowing forecasts to account for events and constraints that the past alone cannot reveal. This requires both reliable numerical forecasting and the ability to interpret contextual information. Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide strong numerical forecasts, while large language models (LLMs) can reason over text, but combining their strengths remains challenging because asking an LLM to generate or revise forecast values directly can distort the temporal structure captured by the TSFM. We instead formulate forecasting as a planning problem over TSFM-generated trajectories. The frozen TSFM acts as a simulator that proposes numerical continuations, while the LLM acts as a policy and value function that guides candidate selection and evaluates completed trajectories against the context. We instantiate this as \rc{} (\textbf{L}LM \textbf{A}s \textbf{F}orecasting \textbf{P}lanner), a training-free framework that bridges the modality gap without retraining either model, using Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) over the forecast horizon with a \emph{Ranker} LLM as policy and a \emph{Judge} LLM as value function. Experiments on Context-is-Key and Time-MMD across two TSFM backbones (Chronos and TimesFM) and four LLMs show that \rc{} delivers consistent improvements across model choices, supporting sequential search as an effective training-free approach to text-conditioned forecasting.

</details>


### [128] [Inverse RL Helps Align AI by Imitating Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24900)
*Michał Wiliński,Liu Leqi,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PARED方法，通过从演示数据中恢复隐式奖励函数作为特征空间的显式函数，利用轻量级判别器区分演示与策略样本，实现无需偏好标注的对齐优化，并在推理重排、在线强化学习及上下文对齐任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法依赖监督微调或基于人工/验证器反馈的强化学习，忽视了仅从演示数据中能否提取可检查、可重用、可在线优化的隐式奖励这一关键问题。受逆强化学习启发，本文旨在探索仅利用演示数据恢复隐式奖励并用于策略对齐。

Method: PARED方法：1) 将隐式奖励定义为响应级特征空间上的显式函数；2) 训练轻量级判别器在特征空间中区分专家演示与策略自身样本；3) 无需偏好标注，演示提供任务监督，可结合AI反馈作为额外监督维度；4) 可用于推理时重排和对抗式在线强化学习优化。

Result: 实验表明：1) 恢复的奖励无需监督损失即可改进基础策略；2) 在标准监督微调后进一步优化可获得额外增益；3) 成功应用于上下文对齐，单一策略可根据不同受众偏好进行定制。

Conclusion: 演示数据本身蕴含可恢复的隐式奖励函数，PARED通过特征空间判别实现了无需偏好标注的对齐，并支持多样化的应用场景如上下文对齐，为语言模型对齐提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Language model alignment aims to make model behavior reliably reflect desirable properties such as helpfulness, safety, and instruction following. Current approaches typically use supervised fine-tuning on demonstrations or reinforcement learning with rewards derived from verifiers or human feedback. These paradigms leave an important question underexplored: can demonstrations alone yield an implicit reward that can be inspected, reused, and optimized on-policy to align AI? Motivated by inverse reinforcement learning, we introduce Projected Alignment Reward Estimated from Demonstrations (PARED). PARED recovers the implicit reward underlying expert demonstrations as an explicit function over a small set of response-level features, learned by a lightweight discriminator that separates demonstrations from the policy's own samples in this feature space. Unlike a standard reward model, PARED requires no task-specific preference annotations: demonstrations provide the task-specific supervision, which can be augmented with AI feedback as additional dimensions of supervision. Through experiments involving inference-time reranking and adversarial on-policy RL, we show that the recovered reward improves a base policy without a supervised loss and yields further gains when optimized after standard supervised fine-tuning. Additionally, we demonstrate that PARED can be used for contextual alignment, in which a single policy can be tailored to the preferences of different audiences.

</details>


### [129] [Multiclass Classification without Labels via Posterior Simplex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24943)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Troels Petersen,Vincenzo Piuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将CWoLa原理从二分类扩展到多分类的无标签分类方法。通过仅利用不同未知混合比例的无标签混合样本，训练分类器区分混合物身份，并利用混合后验空间的(K-1)-单纯形几何结构，通过后验单纯形拟合或瓶颈架构提取潜在类别结构。实验证明该方法在MNIST、CIFAR-10等数据集上能有效恢复潜在类别及其比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实分类问题中可靠实例级标签往往难以获取，但可以通过不同筛选条件、数据源或实验条件构建弱增广无标签样本。现有CWoLa方法仅针对二分类(K=2)场景，缺乏对多分类(K>2)问题的理论扩展和实践方案。

Method: 1) 理论证明多分类混合模型下贝叶斯最优混合分类器g*将数据点映射到混合后验空间中嵌入的(K-1)-单纯形。2) 提出两类无先验方法：后验单纯形拟合（post-hoc simplex fitting）和瓶颈架构（bottleneck architecture）。3) 训练标准分类器区分混合物身份，再从中提取潜在类别结构。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Galaxy10 DECaLS数据集上的实验表明，仅利用混合物身份信息即可恢复潜在类别及其在混合物中的比例，且性能接近全监督方法。

Conclusion: 该方法缩小了弱监督与全监督性能之间的差距，为标签稀缺领域的多类别发现提供了数学上可解释、可扩展的工具。

Abstract: In many classification problems, reliable instance-level labels are unavailable. However, it is often possible to construct weakly enriched unlabeled samples: datasets selected by different cuts, sources, populations, or experimental conditions that change latent class proportions without revealing them. Classification without Labels (CWoLa) shows that, in the binary case ($K=2$), a classifier trained to distinguish two impure mixtures with different class proportions can recover an optimal class discriminator without knowing the mixture proportions. We extend this principle to multiclass learning from several unlabeled mixtures ($K>2$), where the learner observes only mixture identity and neither latent class labels nor class-prior matrices. We prove that, for a multiclass mixture model, the Bayes-optimal mixture classifier $g^\star$ maps data points into a $(K-1)$-simplex embedded in mixture-posterior space. The $K$ vertices of this simplex are induced by the latent classes through the unknown mixing matrix. Leveraging this geometry, we propose prior-free procedures that train a standard classifier to distinguish mixture identities and then extract latent class structure using either post-hoc simplex fitting or a bottleneck architecture. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Galaxy10 DECaLS show that mixture identity alone can recover latent classes and their fractions in the mixture. By narrowing the gap between weakly supervised and fully supervised performance, we provide a mathematically grounded, scalable tool for multiclass discovery in label-scarce domains.

</details>


### [130] [Stable FP4 Training via Transposition-Invariant Block Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24953)
*Mehdi Rahimifar,Amin Darabi,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Xing Huang,Yao Wang,Zhijun Tu,Yufei Cui,Yunke Peng,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于2D块FP4量化的低精度训练框架，通过强制前向-后向缩放一致性解决FP4训练不稳定问题，在7B密集模型和30B MoE模型上实现与BF16相当的性能（困惑度/准确率下降<1.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FP4训练存在不稳定问题，根源在于1D块量化中张量转置导致前向和后向传播对相同值分配不同缩放因子，造成有偏且不稳定的梯度更新。

Method: 提出2D块FP4量化（强制转置不变缩放，保持前后向一致）+ 无截断缩放 + 随机舍入控制量化误差 + 对注意力Q/K投影采用MXFP8量化的混合精度设计。

Result: 在高达7B参数密集LLM和30B MoE模型上，训练至100B tokens，端到端FP4训练稳定，与BF16相比困惑度和下游准确率退化小于1.3%。

Conclusion: 强制前后向缩放一致性足以实现大规模实用FP4训练，为更高效LLM训练提供了简单有效的路径。

Abstract: Reducing training precision is a key lever for improving the e ciency of large language model (LLM) training, but pushing beyond FP8 to 4-bit oating point (FP4) remains challenging due to instability during optimization. We identify a fundamental source of this instability in existing microscaling approaches: scale inconsistency induced by tensor transposition. In conventional 1D block quantization, forward and backward passes assign di erent scaling factors to the same values after transposition, leading to biased and unstable gradient updates. To address this issue, we propose a low-precision training framework based on 2D block FP4 quantization, which enforces transposition-invariant scaling and preserves consistency between forward and backward computations. We further combine this with truncation-free scaling and stochastic rounding to control quantization error and maintain unbiased gradients. To handle the sensitivity of attention mechanisms, we adopt MXFP8 quantization for query and key projections, yielding a practical mixed-precision design. We evaluate our method on dense LLMs up to 7B parameters and a 30B Mixture-of-Experts model, trained on up to 100B tokens. Across all settings, our approach achieves stable end-to-end FP4 training and closely matches BF16 performance, with less than 1.3% degradation in perplexity and downstream accuracy. These results demonstrate that enforcing forwardbackward scaling consistency is su cient to enable practical FP4 training at scale, providing a simple and e ective pathway toward more e cient LLM training.

</details>


### [131] [Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24983)
*Ziwei Zhang,Jonathan Yu-Meng Li,Zhihao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式分布鲁棒优化(GDRO)框架，通过采样器-Sinkhorn配对实现任意条件生成模型下的分布鲁棒优化，无需似然或梯度访问，在库存管理和社交导航任务中显著降低遗憾和碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布鲁棒优化方法在模型兼容性与对抗结构之间存在权衡：接受任意采样器的方法不限制最坏情况分布到生成器族，而生成器参数化的对抗方法依赖于模型特定的似然、分数或训练数据访问。

Method: 提出GDRO框架，核心创新是采样器-Sinkhorn配对：采样器精确表示条件分布，Sinkhorn散度无需似然即可比较分布并仅从样本估计。该问题具有直接有限样本近似和可微原始-对偶实现。

Result: 对于Lipschitz损失，种群Sinkhorn半径约束下游性能下降。在显式和隐式生成器上，该方法将稀有上下文库存遗憾降低60%，SocialGAN导航碰撞减少50%。

Conclusion: GDRO是一个原则性框架，可接受任意可采样的条件生成器作为名义模型，并将最坏情况分布限制在选定的条件生成器族内，在多种生成器类型上均有效。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly adopted in distributionally robust optimization (DRO), but existing approaches trade off model compatibility and adversarial structure: methods that accept arbitrary samplers do not restrict worst-case laws to a generator family, while generator-parameterized adversaries rely on model-specific access such as likelihoods, scores, or training data. We propose Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization (GDRO), a principled framework that accepts any sampleable conditional generator as the nominal model and restricts worst-case laws to a chosen conditional generator family. The key is the sampler-Sinkhorn pairing: samplers represent the conditional laws exactly, while Sinkhorn divergence compares their induced distributions without likelihood access and can be estimated from samples alone. The resulting population problem admits a direct finite-sample approximation and differentiable primal-dual implementation at the active decision context. For Lipschitz losses, the population Sinkhorn radius bounds downstream degradation. Across explicit and implicit generators, our method reduces rare-context inventory regret by 60% and SocialGAN navigation collisions by 50% relative to nominal decisions.

</details>


### [132] [Calibrated Partial Resets: Preventing Policy Collapse in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24996)
*Luc McCutcheon,Evangelos Chatzaroulas,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CPR是一种校准部分重置优化器，通过按神经元效用比例将其向初始值拉动，在不破坏训练稳定性的前提下保持网络可塑性，在持续学习基准上优于现有重置和衰减方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在非平稳数据环境（持续监督学习和强化学习）中面临累积死亡神经元和表达性损失的问题，现有神经元重置方法虽能恢复可塑性但牺牲峰值性能并导致训练不稳定和政策崩塌。

Method: 提出校准部分重置（CPR），周期性地将低效用神经元向初始值拉动，拉动强度由每个神经元的效用校准，避免二元重置的脆弱性和均匀衰减的盲目性。

Result: 在SlipperyAnt上400M训练步中只有CPR避免政策崩塌；在Continual MetaWorld和Continual MinAtar基准上优于先前衰减和重置方法。

Conclusion: 消融实验揭示了可塑性与峰值性能之间的可调权衡，效用标度重初始化是持续学习中有前景的方向。

Abstract: Neural networks are hindered by accumulating dormant neurons and loss of expressivity throughout training, particularly in non-stationary data settings, such as continual supervised and reinforcement learning. Recently, neuron resets have been used to maintain gradient flow and restore plasticity. However, full unit reinitialization often sacrifices peak performance and can destabilize training, leading to policy collapse.
  To preserve plasticity without destabilizing training, we propose Calibrated Partial Resets (CPR), an optimizer that periodically pulls low-utility neurons toward their initialization, with pull strength scaled by each neuron's utility. Unlike binary reset methods, partial resets avoid brittleness; unlike uniform decay, calibrated utility-scaling concentrates adjustment on the units that need it most.
  Among compared methods, only CPR avoids policy collapse over 400M training steps in SlipperyAnt, and it outperforms prior decay and reset-based methods on Continual MetaWorld and Continual MinAtar benchmarks. Ablations reveal a tunable trade-off between plasticity and peak performance, highlighting utility-scaled reinitialization as a promising direction for continual learning.

</details>


### [133] [Conformal Cascade: Distribution-Free Accuracy Guarantees for Multi-Tier LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25018)
*Yifan Dou,Shikan Fang,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Cascade (CC)，一种基于共形预测的多层推理框架，通过预测集大小作为延迟规则，实现对大型语言模型级联推理的分布无关、有限样本准确性保证，无需模型训练，仅需黑盒API访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM级联通过将简单查询路由至小模型、困难查询延迟到大模型来降低推理成本。然而，现有级联系统依赖置信度阈值，但LLM置信度存在校准误差，阈值需针对每对模型和每个领域手动调优，且无法提供级联准确性的形式化保证。

Method: 共形预测集大小作为延迟规则：当校准后的预测集坍缩为单个答案时接受，否则延迟到更大模型。通过逐层联合界，接受层的预测集以至少1-Kα的概率覆盖正确答案；在选择性保持条件下，该界可收紧至1-α。将期望级联成本建模为α和校准集接受率的显式函数。

Result: 在涵盖科学、医学、常识和标准化考试的18个多项选择基准上，使用四个开放权重模型系列的两层级联进行评估，CC在大多数系列-基准对上严格优于最强的校准调优启发式级联，在多数投票不可靠的推理密集型基准上提升最大；在简单基准上，绝大部分查询由小模型处理且无精度损失。

Conclusion: CC提供了分布无关的准确性保证，无需训练和黑盒API访问即可部署，在多数场景下优于传统校准调优方法。扩展到开放式生成需要答案聚类步骤，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) cascades reduce inference cost by routing easy queries to a small model and deferring hard queries to a larger one. Production cascades govern this deferral through a confidence threshold, but LLM confidence scores are miscalibrated, the threshold must be tuned per model pair and per domain, and no setting yields a formal bound on cascade accuracy. We introduce \textbf{Conformal Cascade} (CC), a multi-tier inference framework that uses conformal prediction set size as the deferral rule: accept when the calibrated set collapses to a single answer, defer otherwise. The procedure delivers a distribution-free, finite-sample accuracy guarantee. By a per-tier union bound, the prediction set at the accepting tier covers the correct answer with probability at least $1 - Kα$ for any user-specified $α$; under a selection-preservation condition (consistent with, but not strictly implied by, our marginal coverage results), the bound tightens to $1 - α$. We further characterise expected cascade cost as an explicit function of $α$ and the calibration-set acceptance rate. Across 18 multiple-choice benchmarks spanning science, medicine, commonsense, and standardized exams, evaluated on two-tier cascades drawn from four open-weight model families, CC strictly improves over the strongest calibration-tuned heuristic cascade on the majority of family--benchmark pairs, with the largest gains on reasoning-heavy benchmarks where majority vote is unreliable; on easier benchmarks the cascade commits the vast majority of queries to the small model at no accuracy cost. Extension to open-ended generation requires an answer-clustering step that we leave for future work. The method requires no model training and only black-box API access.

</details>


### [134] [Lantern: Conflict-Aware Gradient Blending for Physics-Guided Diffusion Models in Calorimeter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25060)
*Farzana Yasmin Ahmad,Vanamala Venkataswamy,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lantern，一种结合物理感知辅助损失（方差稳定化体素残差损失和图拉普拉斯损失）与GradBlend梯度混合策略的扩散模型，用于高能物理量热器簇射的快速模拟。在CaloChallenge Dataset 2上，GradBlend相比PCGrad等任务对称方法避免了FPD膨胀2-100倍，同时提升了FPD和CFD指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型用于量热器簇射模拟时，其去噪目标纯统计，可能正确最小化损失但物理错误；现有物理信息生成方法要求封闭形式定律、PDE残差或硬约束，而簇射不具备这些条件（无逐样本PDE，能量守恒仅固定一个标量）。标准指标忽略层间和体素间的相关结构。

Method: 提出Correlation Frobenius Distance (CFD) 作为相关保真度归一化指标。设计两种物理感知辅助损失：方差稳定化体素残差损失（基于计数统计）和图拉普拉斯损失（基于探测器几何）。提出GradBlend梯度混合策略，锚定步长于去噪梯度大小，同时让辅助梯度引导方向。与PCGrad、GradNorm、IMTL-G、ConFIG等任务对称方法对比。

Result: 在CaloChallenge Dataset 2上，PCGrad等任务对称方法注入物理损失后FPD膨胀2-100倍，而GradBlend避免回归且保持性能。结合图拉普拉斯损失的Lantern同时提升FPD和CFD。消融实验表明：体素残差损失与去噪梯度冲突，需终期去噪独占阶段；非冲突的拉普拉斯损失对调度不敏感。

Conclusion: Lantern通过非对称梯度混合策略成功将物理先验融入扩散模型，避免了任务对称方法带来的性能退化，为缺乏封闭形式物理规律的随机级联过程提供了有效的物理引导生成框架。

Abstract: Monte Carlo simulation of calorimeter showers is a principal bottleneck for the High-Luminosity LHC, and diffusion models have emerged as fast, high-fidelity surrogates. Their denoising objective is purely statistical, however: a model can minimize it while placing the physics wrong. Existing physics-informed generative methods cannot close this gap, because they assume a closed-form law, a governing PDE residual or a hard per-sample constraint, that a shower does not supply: no per-sample PDE governs a stochastic cascade, and energy conservation fixes only one scalar per shower. Standard metrics ignore the correlation structure across calorimeter layers and voxels, comparing showers only in a physics feature space. We address both gaps. We introduce the Correlation Frobenius Distance (CFD), a single normalized score for correlation fidelity at layer-wise and voxel-wise scales. We then encode the soft per-sample structure available in a shower as two physics-aware auxiliary losses: a variance-stabilized voxel residual loss grounded in counting statistics, and a graph Laplacian loss over the detector geometry. We combine both with denoising through GradBlend, which anchors the step magnitude to the denoising gradient while letting the auxiliary steer its direction, yielding Lantern, a physics-guided diffusion surrogate. On CaloChallenge Dataset 2, injecting the physics losses through task-symmetric rules such as PCGrad, GradNorm, IMTL-G, and ConFIG inflates FPD by 2-100x relative to denoising alone, whereas GradBlend admits the same signal without regression and, with the Laplacian loss, Lantern improves both FPD and CFD. Our ablation on the auxiliary loss scheduler shows that the voxel residual loss, whose gradient conflicts with denoising, requires a terminal denoising-only phase to preserve shower fidelity, whereas the non-conflicting Laplacian loss is insensitive to the schedule.

</details>


### [135] [Score-Based Stabilization for Time-Dependent Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25119)
*Eshed Gal,Eldad Haber,Uri Ascher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分数(score)的稳定化框架用于偏微分方程数值模拟，通过学习得到的分数模型定义稳定化算子，对数值更新进行校正，使其逼近可容许状态流形，从而增强时间推进格式的稳定性和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值格式在求解偏微分方程时可能产生非物理的不稳定性，需要一种能够保持结构一致性和物理约束的校正机制。

Method: 利用学习得到的分数模型定义稳定化算子，将其作用于标准时间推进格式的临时数值解，通过校正驱动迭代向可容许状态流形收缩。

Result: 在Advection、KdV、NLS和Burgers方程上的数值实验表明，该方法提升了鲁棒性，抑制了非物理不稳定性，并保持了定性动力学特征。

Conclusion: 该稳定化算子具有向可容许流形的收缩性质，形成了一种盆地条件稳定的校正机制，有效增强了数值模拟的物理一致性。

Abstract: We propose a score-based stabilization framework for numerical simulation of partial differential equations, in which a learned score model defines a stabilization operator applied to provisional numerical updates. This operator augments standard time-stepping schemes by enforcing structure and physical consistency through a correction that drives iterates toward the manifold of admissible states. We show that the stabilization operator acts as a contraction toward this manifold, yielding a correction mechanism with basin-conditional stability. Numerical experiments on Advection, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV), Nonlinear Schrodinger (NLS), and Burgers' equations demonstrate improved robustness, suppression of nonphysical instabilities, and preservation of qualitative dynamics.

</details>


### [136] [Semantic Space Search Trajectory Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25122)
*Julian Agudelo,Alberto Tonda,Gabriela Ochoa,Vincent Guigue,Cristina Manfredotti,Evelyne Lutton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在语义空间（模型对固定样本集的预测空间）中构建搜索轨迹网络（STN），通过离散化语义向量并采用基于归一化汉明距离的完全链接聚合聚类来生成网络节点。该方法可在不同算法家族间比较学习动态，并成功应用于分类/回归任务及神经网络泛化研究，发现真实标签训练比随机标签训练产生更密集、高效和中心化的STN结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统STN依赖搜索空间的离散化，主要适用于低维或组合优化问题，难以推广到高维连续空间（如深度学习参数空间）。作者希望将STN扩展到语义空间，以支持对不同机器学习算法（包括深度神经网络）的学习动态进行可视化和比较分析。

Method: 1) 定义语义空间为模型在固定样本集上预测结果的向量空间；2) 对语义向量进行离散化；3) 采用基于归一化汉明距离的完全链接聚合聚类将离散向量聚合成网络节点；4) 构建STN图结构，节点代表聚类后的语义状态，边代表算法在训练过程中的状态转移。

Result: 1) 在分类和回归任务中，语义空间STN能够区分不同机器学习算法的已知定性差异；2) 在神经网络泛化研究中，真实标签训练与随机标签训练产生的STN存在一致的结构差异：真实标签训练产生更密集、更高效、更中心化的图结构。

Conclusion: 语义空间STN能够捕捉学习算法与数据交互产生的功能性训练动态，为跨机器学习模型和训练范式分析和比较学习动态提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Search Trajectory Networks (STNs) are a graph-based tool for visualizing and characterizing the behavior of optimization algorithms. STNs' reliance on discretization of the search space has largely confined them to low-dimensional or combinatorial settings. We introduce a methodology for constructing STNs in semantic spaces, defined as the space of a model's predictions on a fixed sample set. Our approach discretizes semantic vectors and aggregates them into network nodes via agglomerative clustering with complete linkage under a normalized Hamming distance. Since any predictor can be summarized by its semantic vector, this method enables comparison of learning dynamics across otherwise incomparable algorithm families. We apply semantic space STNs to classification and regression tasks solved using different machine learning algorithms, recovering known qualitative differences between them. Additionally, we use semantic space STNs to study neural network generalization by contrasting standard training with the label randomization regime of Zhang et al. (2017). The resulting STNs exhibit consistent structural differences, training on real labels produces denser, more efficient and more centralized graphs than training on shuffled labels. Together, our results show that semantic space STNs capture functional training dynamics arising from the interaction between learning algorithms and data, providing a tool for analyzing and comparing learning dynamics across machine learning models and training regimes.

</details>


### [137] [Endpoint Replay: Compressing the Recency Buffer in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25123)
*Parham Mohammad Panahi,Armin Ashrafi,Haoyu Du,Andrew Patterson,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于n步序列链端点提取的压缩经验回放方法，在保持与大缓冲区相当的有效记忆视野的同时，将存储需求降低一个数量级，并在Pinball和Atari 2600基准测试中匹配了传统大缓冲区的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验回放是深度强化学习中实用且有效的算法工具，但现有方法大多依赖大型均匀采样回放缓冲区（通常大小为100万），存储开销大且可能存在冗余。能否存储更少数据、减少冗余、更有效地链接经验以加速价值传播，同时保持大缓冲区的性能？

Method: 提出一种简单压缩方法：存储从连接的n步序列链端点提取的代表性转移。通过在小回放缓冲区中整理这些端点，该方法保持了与标准大缓冲区相当的有效记忆视野，但存储需求降低一个数量级。

Result: 该方法避免了朴素压缩策略中的系统性偏差，在Pinball环境和Atari 2600基准测试中匹配了传统大缓冲区的性能。

Conclusion: 基于n步序列链端点的压缩经验回放是一种有效的轻量级替代方案，能够在显著降低存储开销的同时，保持与大型经验回放缓冲区相当的性能表现。

Abstract: Experience replay remains one of the most practical and useful algorithmic tools in the deep reinforcement learning (DRL) toolbox. Aside from the limited success of prioritized replay and specialized approaches for large asynchronous systems, most DRL algorithms make use of a large, uniformly sampled recency buffer---even the size, one million, remains unchanged. Could we store less data, reduce redundancy, or more effectively chain experience together to speed up value propagation and still retain the performance of large buffers? In this paper, we investigate a simple compression approach that stores representative transitions derived from the end-points of a chain of connected $n$-step sequences. By curating these end-points in a smaller recency buffer, our method maintains an effective memory horizon comparable to a standard large buffer while requiring an order of magnitude less storage. Through empirical evaluation, we demonstrate that this approach prevents the systematic bias inherent in naive compression strategies and matches the performance of traditional large buffers in the Pinball environment and the Atari 2600 benchmark.

</details>


### [138] [Interpretable GOHR Agents via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25132)
*Shiwei Tan,Yusong Zhao,Weiyi Qin,Wentian Wang,Jacob Feldman,Lazaros K. Gallos,Paul B. Kantor,Vladimir Menkov,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析Transformer智能体在隐藏规则游戏（GOHR）中的内部表征，发现SAE维度能够恢复任务中的关键概念（如形状、桶）以及可解释的策略（如探测假说、负反馈后切换）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解学习型决策系统的内部表征是否包含可解释其行为的概念，特别是在隐含规则推断任务中。

Method: 在GOHR的两规则任务中训练tokenized自回归Transformer智能体，使用SAE对决策token嵌入进行训练，通过概念标签分析SAE维度的选择性。

Result: SAE维度能高度选择性地覆盖对应概念（形状或桶）的决策；个别SAE维度对应可解释策略，如探测某个规则假说并在负反馈后切换。

Conclusion: SAE能够从Transformer智能体的内部表征中恢复任务结构和可解释策略，为理解隐式规则推断提供了可解释性工具。

Abstract: A central challenge in interpreting learned decision-making systems is to determine whether their internal representations contain concepts that help explain their behavior. We report interpretability experiments for a tokenized autoregressive Transformer agent in the Game of Hidden Rules (GOHR). We focus on a compact two-rule task in which both hidden rules map object shapes to target buckets, but with different permutations. The policy is trained on episodes sampled from these two hidden rules and then evaluated with fixed weights. It is never given a rule label and does not use an explicit rule classifier; any rule information must be inferred implicitly from interaction history. In this setting, the correct rule is not identifiable before the agent tries an informative move and observes accept/reject feedback. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) trained on the agent's decision-token embeddings recover this structure. When held-out decisions are labeled by simple concepts such as the chosen shape or bucket, SAE dimensions that are highly selective for a concept cover most decisions where that concept is present. Individual SAE dimensions also correspond to interpretable strategies such as probing one rule hypothesis and switching after negative feedback.

</details>


### [139] [Physics-Informed CNN-LSTM for Street-Scale Urban Flood Prediction: Reconciling Aggregate Accuracy and Street-Level Plausibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25148)
*Luc DCosta,Yidi Wang,Jonathan L. Goodall,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息约束的CNN-LSTM训练框架，通过嵌入重力损失、连续性损失和地形感知虚警惩罚三项可微惩罚项，解决深度学习洪水预测中物理不约束问题。在诺福克洪水数据集上，该模型实现了接近零的重力违例（10^-6量级），街道召回率提升至0.77（基线为0.44），TWI调制惩罚在保持最低MAE的同时恢复了60%的街道召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习代理模型使用均方误差损失训练会产生统计上准确但物理上不约束的洪水预测（如水流向上、自发出现、平滑街道通道），需要将物理约束嵌入训练过程。

Method: 提出物理信息训练框架：在CNN-LSTM模型的损失函数中嵌入三项可微惩罚项：(i) 重力损失（惩罚水位上升违反水面梯度），(ii) 连续性损失（降雨自适应阈值下的局部质量守恒），(iii) 地形感知虚警惩罚（由地形湿度指数TWI调制）。在诺福克洪水数据集（两次风暴事件，300样本）上评估，使用128x128空间网格、15分钟间隔预测。

Result: 物理约束模型实现近零重力违例（10^-6量级），街道召回率0.77±0.09 vs 基线0.44±0.10；优势在留出风暴上翻倍。均匀虚警变体MAE降低16%但街道召回率降至0.25。TWI调制惩罚在所有指标上优于均匀变体，恢复60%街道召回率，MAE在约束变体中最低，街道F1最优。

Conclusion: 揭示了聚合像素级误差与应用特定物理合理性之间的根本张力，地形感知损失调制提供了原则性解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogate models trained with mean-squared-error loss produce statistically accurate but physically unconstrained flood predictions: water may flow uphill, appear spontaneously, or smooth over street-level corridors. We develop a physics-informed training framework for CNN-LSTM models that predict urban flood depths at 15 min intervals over a 128x128 spatial grid. Three differentiable penalty terms are embedded into the loss: (i) a gravity loss penalizing depth increases against the water-surface-elevation gradient, (ii) a continuity loss enforcing local mass conservation with rainfall-adaptive thresholds, and (iii) a topography-aware false-alarm penalty modulated by the topographic wetness index (TWI). We evaluate on the Norfolk, Virginia flood dataset spanning two storm events (August 2017 and September 2022, 300 samples), with all variants trained on identical splits and robustness assessed over repeated random splits and leave-one-storm-out tests. A road-proximal evaluation restricted to a TWI-derived street mask quantifies street-level skill. The physics-constrained model achieves near-zero gravity violations (order 1e-6) and the highest street-channel recall (0.77 +/- 0.09 vs 0.44 +/- 0.10 for the unconstrained baseline), the capability most relevant to traffic routing, and its advantage more than doubles on a held-out storm; a uniform false-alarm variant attains 16% lower mean absolute error but suppresses street recall to 0.25. The TWI-modulated penalty reconciles this trade-off: it improves on the uniform variant on every metric, recovering 60% higher street recall at the lowest MAE among constrained variants and the best street-level F1. These results expose a fundamental tension between aggregate pixel-level error and application-specific physical plausibility, and show that terrain-aware loss modulation offers a principled resolution.

</details>


### [140] [Accurate structural modeling of chemically diverse molecular interfaces with Vilya-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25156)
*Vilya Research,:,Pascal Sturmfels,Naozumi Hiranuma,Milad Salem,Benjamin D. Sellers,Stephen Rettie,CJ San Felipe,Chase A. P. Wood,Jeffrey K. Holden,Adam P. Moyer,Patrick J. Salveson,Ivan Anishchanka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vilya-2是一种基于扩散变换器的全原子结构预测网络，将Vilya-1从单分子建模扩展到蛋白质-肽相互作用建模，通过迁移学习实现对多肽、小分子、大环化合物及微蛋白的高精度结构预测，在肽界面恢复率（59.1%亚2Å RMSD）上远超共折叠模型，并可作为基础模型通过微调用于先导化合物优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于共进化统计的结构预测网络在蛋白质药物发现中取得了成功，但其准确性无法扩展到肽类治疗药物——这类日益重要的药物形式具有非规范残基、大环化及复杂拓扑结构等特征。

Method: 提出Vilya-2扩散变换器，将Vilya-1的全原子表示从单分子建模扩展到蛋白质-靶标相互作用建模；通过全原子表示实现不同分子类型间的迁移学习；生成多样化结构集成并通过校准置信度进行排序。

Result: 在肽界面恢复率上达到59.1%亚2Å骨架RMSD，远超共折叠模型（即使后者以结合态受体为模板）；在小分子对接上达到最先进水平；可泛化到训练中未见的新型蛋白质-小分子复合物；可泛化到比训练分子大数倍的大环化合物和二硫键锁定的微蛋白。

Conclusion: Vilya-2将预测准确性与跨化学空间的广泛泛化能力统一，成为从头肽设计流程所需的结构预测工具，确立了全原子方法作为从头肽治疗药物设计与评估的通用基础。

Abstract: Structure-prediction networks built on co-evolutionary statistics have transformed protein-based drug discovery, yet their accuracy does not extend to peptide therapeutics--an increasingly important modality defined by non-canonical residues, macrocyclization, and complex topologies. We introduce Vilya-2, a diffusion transformer that extends the all-atom representation of Vilya-1 from modeling individual molecules to modeling their interactions with protein targets. This all-atom representation enables transfer learning between different molecular types, and delivers highly accurate structural modeling of peptides across sizes, classes, and compositions bound to therapeutically relevant targets.
